\texttt{Trial \#1: New plan: I got stuck in a loop of trying to make the potion in the kitchen, but I never actually went to the kitchen. Next time, I will first go to the kitchen and then use the cauldron to make the potion with the ingredients and the potion recipe. \\ 
\texttt{Trial \#2: New plan: I got stuck in a loop of trying to take the potion recipe and prickly pear out of the cauldron, but I never actually used the cauldron to make the potion. What is worse, I never actually used the fire wand to heat the cauldron, even though I had it in my inventory. Next time, I will first examine the cauldron to see if it can be used to make the potion, and then use the fire wand to heat it. I will also make sure to use more specific and clear actions, such as "use fire wand to heat cauldron" instead of "use fire wand".} \\
\\ 
\texttt{Trial \#3: New plan: I got stuck in a loop of trying to go to the storage room to get the bucket of water, but I never actually used the bucket of water to make the potion. What is worse, I never actually used the cauldron to make the potion, even though I had all the necessary ingredients. Next time, I will first go to the kitchen and then use the cauldron to make the potion with the ingredients and the potion recipe. I will also make sure to simplify my actions and avoid using ambiguous phrases. } \\
\\ 
\texttt{To achieve this, I will follow these steps:} \\
\\ 
\texttt{1. Go to the kitchen and examine the cauldron to see if it can be used to make the potion.} \\
\texttt{2. Use the fire wand to heat the cauldron.} \\
\texttt{3. Use the ladle to scoop water from the bucket of water in the storage room.} \\
\texttt{4. Pour the water into the cauldron.} \\
\texttt{5. Use the potion recipe to make the potion in the cauldron.} \\
\\ 
\texttt{I will also make sure to use more specific and clear actions, such as "use fire wand to heat cauldron" instead of "use fire wand".} \\
\\ 
\\
